    方铁矿框架中的受挫非共线磁性：β-Fe2O3基态#
    The bixbyite framework as a platform for frustrated noncollinear magnetism: resolving the magnetic ground state of β-Fe₂O₃#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Chenjun Tang (Institut de Ciència de Materials de Barcelona), Ondřej Malina (Regional Centre of Advanced Technologies and Materials. CATRIN. Olomouc. Czech Republic, Nanotechnology Centre-CEET VSB-TUO. Ostrava. Poruba, Czech Republic), Jiři Tuček (Research Centre of FEEI-UPCE. Pardubice. Czech Republic), Francois Fauth (CELLS-ALBA Synchrotron. Cerdanyola del Vallès), Marti Gich (Institut de Ciència de Materials de Barcelona), Jose Luis Garcia-Muñoz (Institut de Ciència de Materials de Barcelona)
    相关度
    5

    📄 摘要：通过中子和同步辐射X射线衍射确定β-Fe2O3的磁基态。奈尔温度以下出现非共线反铁磁态，由H点mH₁⁺不可约表示驱动。作者将强受挫归因于方铁矿结构对交换拓扑和磁各向异性的共同影响，揭示同成分Fe2O3多晶型磁性差异的结构来源。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2606.17842v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Magnetic behavior across Fe₂O₃ polymorphs varies widely despite identical chemistry, highlighting crystal architecture as a key determinant of exchange topology, magnetic anisotropy, and ultimately magnetic order. Here, using neutron and synchrotron X-ray diffraction, we establish the magnetic ground state of the poorly understood bixbyite β-Fe₂O₃ polymorph and uncover the structural origin of its strong frustration. Below the Néel temperature, a noncollinear antiferromagnetic state emerges through activation of the mH₁⁺ irrep at the H-point [k=(1,1,1)] and the antitranslation (1'|tfrac12,tfrac12,tfrac12), breaking the body centering and yielding two interpenetrating primitive cubic magnetic subcells with inverted moments and nonpolar type-IV symmetry. Under exclusively antiferromagnetic Fe³⁺-O-Fe³⁺ interactions, β-Fe₂O₃ exhibits a large frustration index (f approx 7.56). This behavior originates from the intrinsic geometry of the bixbyite lattice: two magnetic sublattices with distinct point symmetries and anisotropy constraints are embedded in a three-dimensional exchange network containing interconnected triangular and hexagonal motifs. In 111 planes, Fe2 ions form hexagonal rings interconnected by frustrated triangular units, while locally Ising-like Fe1 ions occupy the ring centers. Our results thus identify the bixbyite architecture as a promising general platform for frustrated noncollinear magnetism. Extending this structural framework to other magnetic transition-metal or 4f ions opens a materials space for engineering competing exchange interactions and anisotropies, potentially stabilizing new noncollinear and field-tunable magnetic states.

    💡 亮点：文章用中子和同步辐射X射线衍射解析了此前不清楚的β-Fe2O3磁基态。实验发现奈尔温度以下由H点mH₁⁺不可约表示激活的非共线反铁磁序，并把强受挫联系到方铁矿框架的交换拓扑与各向异性。摘要没有给出奈尔温度或磁矩角度。该结果直接对应团队的非共线磁性、群论和自旋哈密顿量研究。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及铁电/多铁、极化翻转和电场驱动过程, 磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    一维环中准周期性、Hubbard相互作用与交错磁性#
    Interplay of Quasiperiodicity, Hubbard Interactions, and Staggered Magnetism in a 1D Ring: Localization and Re-entrant Characteristics#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Souvik Roy, Ranjini Bhattacharya
    相关度
    3

    📄 摘要：采用自洽Hartree-Fock方法研究自旋ful准周期Hubbard环，其中跃迁幅准周期调制并施加交错自旋依赖Zeeman场。作者用逆参与率、归一化参与率和多重分形分析定位化，并考察双占据、密度涨落、局域熵和自旋密度波等实空间观测量，发现局域化具有再入特征。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2603.29177v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We study interaction-induced localization in a spinful quasiperiodic Hubbard ring with quasiperiodically modulated hopping and a staggered spin-dependent Zeeman field using a self-consistent Hartree-Fock approach. We characterize localization through the inverse participation ratio, normalized participation ratio, and multifractal analysis, and further examine its signatures in self-consistent real-space observables, including double occupancy, density fluctuations, local entropy, spin-density-wave order, and related correlation measures. As the Hubbard interaction is increased, we find a nonmonotonic evolution of the eigenstates: an initially extended regime gives way to an intermediate localized phase, followed by reentrant delocalization at stronger interactions. The width of this intermediate regime grows systematically with both the quasiperiodic hopping amplitude and the staggered Zeeman-field strength, indicating a cooperative enhancement of localization by these two ingredients. In the localized regime, the self-consistent interaction produces pronounced spin dependence in both the spectra and localization properties, resulting in distinct responses of the spin-up and spin-down sectors. The interaction-driven crossover is consistently captured by the real-space observables and further corroborated by real-time wave-packet dynamics, which reveal successive ballistic, confined, and reentrant-transport regimes consistent with the underlying eigenstate character. These results provide a unified framework for understanding the interplay of quasiperiodicity, electronic interactions, and spin-dependent fields in controlling localization, criticality, and quantum transport in correlated quasiperiodic systems.

    💡 亮点：工作研究准周期跃迁、Hubbard相互作用和交错自旋场共同作用下的一维自旋ful环。自洽Hartree-Fock结合逆参与率、归一化参与率、多重分形及双占据等实空间量，刻画相互作用诱导局域化和再入行为。摘要没有给出临界耦合或系统尺寸。它与团队有效哈密顿量、磁性模型和输运研究存在理论方法交叉，但不是具体材料问题。

    Two-dimensional quantum Griffith singularity in three-dimensional ZrNₓ superconducting films#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Zi-Yan Han, Li-Min Yu, Yu-Cheng Cong, Yang Yang, Zhi-Xiang Sun, Zhi-Qing Li🔥 AI 82
    相关度
    3

    📄 摘要：我们在约 200 nm 厚的外延 ZrNₓ 超导薄膜中报告了二维（2D）量子 Griffith 奇异性（QGS）的实验观测结果。这些薄膜具有岩盐结构，并且就超导性而言属于三维（3D）体系。对于每一片 x 大于或约等于 1.30 的薄膜，在低温下，垂直于薄膜平面和平行于薄膜平面的磁场所对应的磁阻等温线，会在一个较宽的磁场范围内分别发生交叉，而不是在单一交叉点处交叉。尽管超导性在宏观上具有三维性质，但选定的相邻温度下的磁阻等温线遵循二维超导系统幂律标度的理论预言，而不是三维系统的理论预言。通过使用二维幂律标度分析磁阻等温线得到的有效临界指数 zν 随温度降低而增大，并在接近量子相变时发散。此外，超导体-绝缘体或超导体-金属转变附近的电阻率数据遵循一种激活标度形式，该形式描述了由无穷随机性临界点支配的二维超导系统量子相变。ZrNₓ 薄膜中的 QGS 被归因于本征缺陷（例如 Zr 空位和 N 间隙原子）所诱导的淬灭无序，这些缺陷形成了空间上不均匀的超导稀有区域。这些稀有区域的动力学在量子临界点附近可能表现出有效的二维特征，并在量子相变附近主导体系的输运性质。我们的结果为三维超导体中存在 QGS 提供了有力证据，并突出了无序诱导的非均匀性在决定量子相变临界行为中的关键作用。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28166v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We report the experimental observation of two-dimensional (2D) quantum Griffiths singularity (QGS) in sim200-nm-thick epitaxial ZrNₓ superconducting films. The films possess a rock-salt structure and are three-dimensional (3D) with respect to superconductivity. For each film with x gtrsim 1.30, the low-temperature magnetoresistance isotherms under fields perpendicular and parallel to the film plane cross over at a broad magnetic field range independently rather than at a single crossing point. Despite the macroscopic 3D nature of the superconductivity, the magnetoresistance isotherms at selected adjacent temperatures follow the theoretical prediction of power-law scaling for 2D superconducting systems, rather than that for 3D systems. The effective critical exponent zν, obtained by analyzing the magnetoresistance isotherms using the 2D power-law scaling, increases with decreasing temperature and diverges as the quantum phase transition is approached. In addition, the resistivity data near the superconductor-insulator or superconductor-metal transitions obey an activated scaling form that describes the quantum phase transition of 2D superconducting systems governed by an infinite-randomness critical point. The QGS in the ZrNₓ films is attributed to quenched disorder induced by intrinsic defects, such as Zr vacancies and N interstitials, which creates spatially inhomogeneous superconducting rare regions. The dynamics of these rare regions, which may exhibit effective 2D characteristics near the quantum critical point, dominate the transport properties of the system near the quantum phase transition. Our results provide compelling evidence for the existence of QGS in 3D superconductors and highlight the crucial role of disorder-induced inhomogeneity in determining the critical behavior of quantum phase transitions.

    💡 亮点：实验在约200 nm外延ZrNx薄膜中发现二维量子Griffith奇异性，同时指出其超导宏观维度仍为三维。x≥约1.30时，垂直和平行磁场下磁阻等温线在宽场区间交叉，区别于单一量子临界点。该工作提供了无序超导薄膜中维度交叉的输运证据。与团队超导和电子输运方向相关，但不是材料模拟或AI方法论文。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 缺陷、畴壁、成核和开关动力学对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    Nagaoka铁磁体的坏金属行为与Lifshitz相变#
    Bad Metal Behavior and Lifshitz Transition of a Nagaoka Ferromagnet#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jonas Arnold, Peter Kopietz, Andreas Rückriegel
    相关度
    7.5

    📄 摘要：作者扩展费米子泛函重整化群以处理投影Hilbert空间中的强关联，并研究无限现场排斥Hubbard模型。方格晶格最近邻跃迁下，低密度顺磁费米液体经中等密度反铁磁条纹序，演化至高密度扩展Nagaoka铁磁态，同时计算电子谱函数并揭示坏金属行为和Lifshitz转变。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2510.01909v4 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Using an extension of the fermionic functional renormalization group for systems where strong correlations give rise to projected Hilbert spaces we calculate the phase diagram and the electronic spectral function of the Hubbard model at infinite on-site repulsion. For a square lattice with nearest-neighbor hopping we find that the ground state evolves from a paramagnetic Fermi liquid at low densities via a state with antiferromagnetic stripe order at intermediate densities to an extended Nagaoka ferromagnet at high densities. The single-particle spectral function of the Nagaoka ferromagnet exhibits a flat but rather broad band characteristic for an incoherent non-Fermi liquid. We identify two distinct ferromagnetic regimes separated by a Lifshitz transition.

    💡 亮点：工作用适用于投影Hilbert空间的费米子泛函重整化群计算无限现场排斥Hubbard模型的相图和单粒子谱。方格晶格中体系从低密度顺磁费米液体，经反铁磁条纹序，进入高密度扩展Nagaoka铁磁态，并伴随坏金属和Lifshitz转变。摘要未给出密度临界值或谱函数数值。它与团队强关联磁性和有效模型研究相关，但离具体材料较远。

    结合多智能体强化学习的低空流体天线网络#
    Low-Altitude Fluid Antenna Network with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cs.AI👤 Tong Zhang, Yanfei Su, Shuai Wang, Wanli Ni, Chengzhong Xu, Huseyin Arslan🔥 AI 72
    相关度
    7

    📄 摘要：针对低空无线网络中的动态空地信道、突发遮挡和异构干扰，作者研究流体天线网络与多智能体强化学习的联合方案。流体天线通过重构天线位置改变多输入多输出信道，多智能体强化学习用于动态协调通信资源。摘要未给出状态空间、奖励函数、智能体数量或性能增益。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27909v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs) integrate terrestrial and aerial platforms to provide ubiquitous communication, sensing, and localization services for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and electric vertical takeoff and landing (eVTOL) aircraft. However, dynamic air-ground and air-air channels, abrupt blockages, and heterogeneous interference hinder the realization of this goal. Nevertheless, fluid antenna (FA), a cutting-edge multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) technique, overcomes these challenges by reconfiguring antenna positions to unlock additional spatial degrees-of-freedom. In this paper, towards bringing low-altitude FA networks into reality, we study the fast and high-performance FA reconfiguration for low-altitude FA networks with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Specifically, we present an electromagnetic digital twin (EM-DT)-assisted MARL framework. To fill the sim-to-real gap, we introduce a two-stage transfer learning framework. Our case study shows that joint FA positions and beamforming optimization can enhance the system sum-rate by 118.5%, compared to the fixed position baseline. This gain comes from the dynamic millisecond timescale reconfiguration of FA arrays and the adaptive steering of beams toward aerial users with mobility.

    💡 亮点：文章把可重构位置的流体天线与多智能体强化学习结合，用于低空空地网络中的动态通信控制。问题包含空中平台移动、遮挡、空空干扰和天线位置决策。摘要没有给出网络架构、训练算法、信道参数或吞吐量提升。它与团队AI for science的强化学习控制在算法范式上相关，但与凝聚态材料、物理模拟和材料性质预测没有直接交叉。

    Fe/GaAs(110)薄膜磁弹性与磁各向异性深度分布#
    Magnetoelasticity in Fe/GaAs(110) films: Depth profile of magnetic anisotropy#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Editors' Suggestions👤 Aleksandra Lindner, Rodolfo A. Gallardo, Andreas Henschke, Fabian Ganss, Javier Pablo-Navarro, Gabriel Gray, Ruslan Salikhov, Kilian Lenz, Toni Hache, Dirk Sander, Gauravkumar Patel, Sebastian Fähler, Olav Hellwig, Jürgen Fassbender, and Jürgen Lindner🔥 AI 83
    相关度

    📄 摘要：作者：Aleksandra Lindner、Rodolfo A. Gallardo、Andreas Henschke、Fabian Ganss、Javier Pablo-Navarro、Gabriel Gray、Ruslan Salikhov、Kilian Lenz、Toni Hache、Dirk Sander、Gauravkumar Patel、Sebastian Fähler、Olav Hellwig、Jürgen Fassbender 和 Jürgen Lindner。作者在此报道，在厚外延 bcc Fe(110)/GaAs(110) 薄膜中，立方磁各向异性和单轴磁各向异性共存，后者的量级可与前者相当。它们之间的相互作用稳定了沿 ⟨001⟩ 方向的面内易轴，对于厚度最高达 100 nm 的薄膜，这一易轴贯穿整个膜体积而保持存在。这种非常规行为导致了随深度变化的磁响应，垂直驻波自旋波模式对薄膜厚度不同区域表现出不同的敏感性。该研究进一步确定，各向异性应变是观测到的面内单轴磁各向异性的微观起源。[《Physical Review B》114，094438]，发表于 2026 年 8 月 31 日。

    📖 英文原文

    Author(s): Aleksandra Lindner, Rodolfo A. Gallardo, Andreas Henschke, Fabian Ganss, Javier Pablo-Navarro, Gabriel Gray, Ruslan Salikhov, Kilian Lenz, Toni Hache, Dirk Sander, Gauravkumar Patel, Sebastian Fähler, Olav Hellwig, Jürgen Fassbender, and Jürgen Lindner The authors report here the coexistence of cubic and uniaxial magnetic anisotropies in thick epitaxial bcc Fe(110)/GaAs(110) films, with the latter having a magnitude comparable to that of the former. Their interplay stabilizes an in-plane easy axis along the ⟨001⟩ directions that persists throughout the film volume for thicknesses up to 100 nm. This unconventional behavior gives rise to a depth-dependent magnetic response, with perpendicular standing spin-wave modes exhibiting distinct sensitivities to different regions of the film thickness. The study further identifies anisotropic strain as the microscopic origin of the observed in-plane uniaxial anisotropy. [Phys. Rev. B 114, 094438] Published Mon Aug 31, 2026

    💡 亮点：工作揭示外延Fe(110)/GaAs(110)薄膜中立方与单轴磁各向异性共存，并通过竞争稳定面内〈001〉易轴。单轴项与立方项量级相当，且易轴沿厚度方向保持。摘要没有给出厚度上限、各向异性能量或应变数值。它与团队的磁弹性、自旋-晶格耦合、界面效应和磁性薄膜建模直接相关。

    改进Hasegawa-Wakatani模型中的强化学习湍流转变控制#
    Reinforcement-learning control of turbulence transition in the modified Hasegawa-Wakatani system#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · AI×物理📖 arXiv / physics👤 Luning Sun, Ben Zhu, Xin-Yang Liu, Deepak Akhare, Jian-Xun Wang🔥 AI 100
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：在改进Hasegawa-Wakatani模型中用强化学习实现湍流与区带流转变的双向控制。模型加入弱区带阻尼，使长寿命区带结构具有有限寿命，恢复驱动—阻尼平衡，从而可在有限控制回合内重复转变。摘要未给出智能体架构、奖励函数或控制成功率。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27845v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Control of plasma turbulence remains a long-standing challenge in magnetically confined fusion research. Here, we explore a reinforcement-learning (RL) approach for bidirectional control of the turbulence-zonal-flow transition in the modified Hasegawa-Wakatani system, a minimal model of electrostatic drift-wave turbulence. Within our model, a weak zonal drag damps the otherwise long-lived zonal structures, giving them a finite lifetime and restoring the drive-damping balance required for repeatable transitions within finite control episodes; meanwhile, actuation is applied through a spatially distributed Gaussian source field under a time-weighted budget constraint on actuation costs. The plasma model is then coupled to CNN-based soft actor-critic and twin-delayed deterministic policy-gradient agents through a GPU-native JAX solver that is highly optimized for fast online training. In the turbulence-suppression task, the learned budget-aware schedule achieves the lowest time-integrated turbulent flux for a given consumed budget, outperforming both constant and linearly decreasing baselines across all tested unseen initial conditions. In the inverse zonal-break task, the agent discovers an up-down antisymmetric actuation pattern that induces radial E× B convection, disrupts the zonal structure, and sustains the turbulent state. A physics-informed warm-buffer initialization facilitates this discovery, as random exploration alone struggles to locate the narrow optimal manifold within the vast action space. These results demonstrate reinforcement learning as a practical trajectory optimizer for nonlinear plasma dynamics, such as turbulence control, and highlight the importance of physics guidance in such applications.

    💡 亮点：工作在改进Hasegawa-Wakatani漂移波模型中训练强化学习控制器，实现湍流—区带流转变的双向操控。关键建模处理是加入弱区带阻尼，使区带结构寿命有限并恢复可重复的驱动—阻尼平衡。摘要没有给出网络结构、训练步数或成功率。它与团队发展AI控制复杂非平衡动力学的方向直接相关，可为极化、自旋和载流子动力学控制提供算法基准。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：强化学习和闭环控制，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    双层镍酸盐La3Ni2O7中层间自旋单态与SDW序的轨道选择性共存#
    Orbital-Selective Coexistence of Interlayer Spin-Singlet Formation and SDW Order with Anomalous Spin Reconfiguration in Bilayer Nickelate La₃Ni₂O₇ Revealed by ¹⁷O-NMR#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 H. Lee, M. Yashima, M. Kakoi, T. Ino, Y. Arai, K. Kitagawa, H. Sakurai, Y. Takano, K. Kuroki, H. Mukuda🔥 AI 87
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：研究采用对内顶位O(1)、外顶位O(2)和面内O(3,4)位点进行选择性17O核磁共振测量，考察了双层镍酸盐La3Ni2O7中自旋密度波（SDW）有序的自旋结构。在TSDW=150 K以下，所有面内O(3,4)位点的谱峰均因有限内部磁场的出现而显著展宽，而O(2)位点则没有内部磁场，或仅存在可忽略不计的内部磁场。这些结果与具有单条无自旋（或大-小自旋）条纹的公度SDW有序相一致。对于连接NiO2平面的O(1)位点，其内部磁场在TSDW以下几乎完全抵消，表明相邻平面之间存在反平行自旋构型。然而，在TA（约115 K）以下，尽管面内SDW有序仍然稳健，O(1)位点的谱线却消失了，这意味着尽管预期NiO2平面之间存在很强的层间自旋耦合，经由Ni-O(1)-Ni键的反平行自旋构型在TA以下并不特别稳定。尤为重要的是，我们强调，与d3z2-r2轨道具有强共价性的O(2)位点处局域自旋磁化率极低，这表明连接NiO2平面的Ni-d3z2-r2轨道中形成了充分发展的层间自旋单态。这些发现为理解层间自旋单态形成，以及通过连接NiO2平面的Ni-O(1)-Ni成键轨道发生的异常自旋重构提供了新视角；这些现象表征了双层镍酸盐La3Ni2O7的轨道选择性本质。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27917v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The spin structure of the spin density wave (SDW) order in the bilayer nickelate La₃Ni₂O₇ has been investigated using site-selective ¹⁷O-NMR measurements on the inner apical O(1), outer apical O(2), and planar O(3,4) sites. Below Tₘ̊ SDW (= 150 K), the peak of all planar O(3,4) sites significantly broadens due to the emergence of a finite internal magnetic field, whereas O(2) sites remain with no (or a negligibly small) internal field. These results are consistent with commensurate SDW order with a single spin-spinless (or large-tiny spin) stripe. As for the O(1) sites that bridge the NiO₂ planes, the internal field is nearly canceled below Tₘ̊ SDW, indicating an antiparallel spin configuration between adjacent planes. However, below T_A (sim 115 K), the spectrum of the O(1) site disappears even though the in-plane SDW order remains robust, implying that the antiparallel spin configuration through the Ni--O(1)--Ni bond is not particularly stable below Tₘ̊ A, despite the expected strong interlayer spin coupling between the NiO₂ planes. Above all, we emphasize that the local spin susceptibility is extremely small at the O(2) site that has a strong covalency with the d₃z^₂₋ᵣ^₂ orbital, indicating a well-developed interlayer spin-singlet formation in the Ni-d₃z^₂₋ᵣ^₂ orbitals bridging the NiO₂ planes. These findings shed new light on the interlayer spin-singlet formation and the anomalous spin reconfiguration through the Ni--O(1)--Ni bonding orbitals connecting the NiO₂ planes, which characterize the orbital-selective nature of the bilayer nickelate La₃Ni₂O₇.

    💡 亮点：17O核磁共振区分了La3Ni2O7内顶位、外顶位和平面氧位点的局域磁场响应。150 K以下平面氧谱展宽而O(2)几乎不受影响，指向轨道选择性的层间自旋单态共存与条纹型SDW结构。该工作提供了局域磁序判别约束，对团队研究磁性、有效自旋哈密顿量和超导相关电子结构具有直接数据参照，但摘要未包含计算或机器学习方法。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    线性上下文学习中淬火临界性的朗道理论#
    Landau theory of quenched criticality in linear in-context learning#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Daesik Kim, Sumin Choi, Hyojae Jeon, Jung Hoon Han🔥 AI 86
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：上下文学习（ICL）使预训练模型能够从提示词中给出的示例推断新任务，而无需更新其参数。在ICL的线性模型中，当预训练样本数与可学习参数数目相当时，预测误差会出现双降奇异性。我们将这种插值奇异性表述为淬火无序系统的临界现象。通过比较同一线性ICL模型的退火描述和淬火描述，我们识别出学习参数的样本间连通涨落是奇异误差的微观起源。通过积分重整化岭参数ξ的腔体自洽方程，构造了一个朗道势。序参量，即类似于磁化强度的量，由ξ承担，而裸岭参数λ成为其共轭磁场。归一化样本复杂度τ充当温度，双降奇异性发生在临界温度τc=1。朗道磁化率恰好就是在预测误差涨落贡献中发散的量。在无岭极限下，序参量与经验弛豫矩阵零本征值所占比例密切相关，而这些零本征值定义了学习动力学中的平坦方向。该朗道理论在序参量中一般为三次形式，其临界指数为(βcr,δcr,γcr)=(1,2,1)。在大上下文区间内，出现了一个以序参量受抑制为特征的赝能隙式区域。朗道理论的预测通过原始学习问题数值解得到了独立确认，并具有良好的定量一致性。我们的结果为扎实地从统计物理学角度理解线性上下文学习中的插值临界性开辟了道路。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28059v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) allows a pretrained model to infer a new task from examples supplied in its prompt without updating its parameters. In linear models of ICL, the prediction error develops a double-descent singularity when the number of pretraining samples becomes comparable to the number of learnable parameters. We formulate this interpolation singularity as a critical phenomenon of a quenched disordered system. By comparing annealed and quenched descriptions of the same linear ICL model, we identify the connected sample-to-sample fluctuations of the learned parameters as the microscopic origin of the singular error. A Landau potential is constructed by integrating the cavity self-consistency equation for the renormalized ridge parameter ξ. The role of (magnetization) order parameter is played by ξ, while the bare ridge parameter łambda becomes its conjugate magnetic field. The normalized sample complexity τ acts as a temperature and the double-descent singularity occurs at the critical temperature τ_c =1. The Landau susceptibility is precisely the quantity that diverges in the fluctuation contribution to the prediction error. The order parameter is closely related to the fraction of zero eigenvalues of the empirical relaxation matrix in the ridgeless limit, which define flat directions in the learning dynamics. The Landau theory is generically cubic in the order parameter with critical exponents (βₘ̊ cᵣ,δₘ̊ cᵣ,γₘ̊ cᵣ)=(1,2,1). In the large-context regime, there appears a pseudogap-like regime characterized by suppressed order parameter. Predictions of the Landau theory are independently confirmed from numerical solutions of the original learning problem with good quantitative agreement. Our results pave the way for solid statistical-physics understanding of the interpolation criticality in linear in-context learning.

    💡 亮点：作者把线性上下文学习的双下降奇异性改写为淬火无序系统的临界现象，并比较退火与淬火平均以分离样本到样本涨落。核心控制量是预训练样本数与可学习参数数目的相对规模，临界区对应插值条件。该理论与团队材料机器学习中的泛化、主动学习和数据分布迁移有方法论交叉，但不涉及具体材料、电子结构或物理对称性。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    射频超导腔表面耗散的三流体模型#
    A Three-Fluid Model of Dissipation at Surfaces in Superconducting Radiofrequency Cavities#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Matthias Liepe Michelle M. Kelley, Sean Deyo, Nathan Sitaraman, Danilo B. Liarte, Tomás Arias, James P. Sethna, Thomas Oseroff🔥 AI 6
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：针对超导腔强射频场下质量因子随场幅增加而改善的反Q斜率，数值求解平行磁场中超导表面的Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程。高场下出现低于体超导能隙、随场循环产生和消失的表面准粒子态，并据此将标准两流体模型扩展为三流体模型。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28565v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Experiments on superconducting cavities have found that under large RF fields the quality factor can improve with increasing field amplitude, a so-called ``anti-Q slope.'' We numerically solve the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations at a superconducting surface in a parallel magnetic field, finding at large fields there are surface quasiparticle states with energies below the bulk superconducting gap that emerge and disappear as the field cycles. Modifying the standard two-fluid model, we introduce a ``three''-fluid model where we partition the normal fluid to consider continuum and surface quasiparticle states separately. We compute dissipation in a semi-classical theory of conductivity, where we provide physical estimates of elastic scattering times of Bogoliubov quasiparticles with point-like impurities having potential strengths informed from complementary ab initio calculations of impurities in bulk niobium. We show, in this simple yet effective framework, how the relative scattering rates of surface and continuum quasiparticle states can play a role in producing an anti-Q slope while demonstrating how this model naturally includes a mechanism for turning the anti-Q slope on and off.

    💡 亮点：BdG表面计算显示，强射频磁场可诱导低于体能隙的表面准粒子态，并在场循环中生成和消失，从而为超导腔反Q斜率提出三流体耗散描述。模型把正常组分进一步分区，以捕捉表面态贡献。该工作与团队在超导、电子-声子耦合和BdG方法方面直接相关，但摘要未涉及具体材料缺陷、界面或机器学习加速。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    通量工程Lieb晶格中的自旋选择性谱展平与波包动力学#
    Spin-Selective Spectral Flattening and Wave-Packet Dynamics in a Flux-Engineered Lieb Lattice#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Nana Chang, Xiaoji Zhou, Yanglin Zhou, Song Ci🔥 AI 96
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们研究了二维最近邻Lieb晶格中由自旋依赖Peierls相位诱导的、可逆的内态选择性波包输运。两个守恒自旋分量经历的有效通量为ασ=α0+sσασ，其中s上、下=±1。在工作点α0=ασ=1/4时，自旋向上和自旋向下分量分别经历α上=1/2和α下=0。在q=2磁性元胞中进行的分辨能带计算表明，自旋向上谱包含两个与子晶格不平衡平带扇区相关的零能平坦子带，而其余四个子带仍保有有限带宽。因此，半通量扇区不满足所有能带均平坦的条件，也无法对一般局域初态实现精确的Aharonov-Bohm笼禁。尽管如此，实时模拟揭示出，相对于色散性的自旋向下分量，自旋向上传播受到显著抑制，表现为在反射前时间窗口内较小的均方位移和较高的有限区域滞留概率。反转态依赖通量会交换慢速与快速自旋通道，而动力学对比对于适度的通量失谐仍然稳健。这些结果确立了自旋依赖人工通量作为一种可逆手段，可在无自旋翻转过程或相互作用的条件下控制按内态分辨的物质波输运，并为在原子和光子平带模拟器中区分精确笼禁与有限时间动力学减速提供了互补的谱学和实空间判据。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28054v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We investigate reversible internal-state-selective wave-packet transport induced by spin-dependent Peierls phases in a two-dimensional nearest-neighbor Lieb lattice. The two conserved spin components experience effective fluxes ασ=α₀+sσαₛ, where sₚ̆ₐᵣᵣₒw,dₒwₙₐᵣᵣₒw=±1. At the working point α₀=αₛ=1/4, the spin-up and spin-down components experience αₚ̆ₐᵣᵣₒw=1/2 and αdₒwₙₐᵣᵣₒw=0, respectively. A band-resolved calculation in the q=2 magnetic unit cell shows that the spin-up spectrum contains two zero-energy flat subbands associated with the sublattice-imbalance flat-band sector, whereas the remaining four subbands retain finite bandwidths. The half-flux sector therefore fails the all-bands-flat condition and does not realize exact Aharonov--Bohm caging for a generic localized initial state. Nevertheless, real-time simulations reveal a pronounced suppression of spin-up propagation relative to the dispersive spin-down component, manifested by a smaller mean-square displacement and an enhanced finite-region retention probability over the pre-reflection time window. Reversing the state-dependent flux interchanges the slow and fast spin channels, while the dynamical contrast remains robust against moderate flux detuning. These results establish spin-dependent synthetic flux as a reversible means of controlling internal-state-resolved matter-wave transport without spin-flip processes or interactions, and provide complementary spectral and real-space criteria for distinguishing exact caging from finite-time dynamical slowing in atomic and photonic flat-band simulators.

    💡 亮点：工作以自旋依赖Peierls相位实现Lieb晶格中两种自旋分量的差异化输运，在α0=αs=1/4时分别对应半通量和零通量。q=2磁性元胞计算显示自旋向上出现两个零能平带，并分析其波包动力学。该结果直接涉及人工晶格中的自旋选择性平带与可逆操控，对团队研究自旋哈密顿量、非共线磁性和拓扑功能材料具有模型层面的参考价值。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    将塞曼劈裂转化为双量子点中的可切换电荷极化#
    Turning Zeeman splitting into switchable charge polarization in a double quantum dot#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Chi Sun, Jacob Linder🔥 AI 82
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：研究均匀塞曼场如何重构并反转非对称开放双量子点中的单电子电荷极化。采用保持对称性的格林函数运动方程方法，在单电子电荷扇区内识别优选量子点占据反转的参数区域，并指出不等栅极能级等两种机制。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27625v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: A magnetic field that acts identically on two quantum dots is not expected to move charge between them. Nevertheless, we show that a uniform Zeeman field can strongly reconfigure and even reverse the single-electron charge polarization of an asymmetric open double quantum dot. Using a symmetry-preserving Green's-function equation-of-motion approach, we identify regimes where the preferred dot occupation reverses while the system remains in the single-electron charge sector. Two distinct mechanisms produce this behavior. Unequal gate levels produce different occupation responses because the Zeeman-shifted resonances lie at different positions relative to the reservoir chemical potential, whereas unequal onsite interactions distinguish the dots through their many-body addition spectra. Coulomb blockade stabilizes the single-electron sector, causing the reservoir-mediated response to appear as spatial charge redistribution rather than a change in total occupation. Our results establish a mechanism for magnetic control of charge polarization at fixed electrostatic detuning.

    💡 亮点：均匀磁场通常不产生双量子点间电荷转移，但在非对称开放体系中可通过塞曼劈裂反转单电子占据偏极化。作者使用保持对称性的格林函数运动方程方法，分析不等栅极能级等机制，并限定在单电子电荷扇区。该结果与团队研究自旋-电荷耦合、量子器件和磁场调控功能材料存在概念交叉，尚未连接到具体材料计算。

    三维U(1)规范理论的另一种连续极限#
    A different kind of continuum limit for the three-dimensional U(1) gauge theory#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Andreas Athenodorou, Claudio Bonati, Ivan Soler Calero🔥 AI 6
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：研究三维紧致U(1)晶格规范理论中通过单极子动力学理解禁闭的问题。标准Wilson作用量的连续极限存在胶球质量与弦张力平方根之比趋于零的病态；通过引入耦合单极子总数的参数μ，同时调节β和μ，寻找保持物理量不变的路径。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.27976v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: In three-dimensional compact U(1) lattice gauge theory color confinement can be understood analytically through the dynamics of magnetic monopoles. However, its continuum limit is pathological, since the ratio of the glueball masses to the square root of the string tension vanishes as the continuum limit is approached. We investigate a simple extension of the Wilson action in which the total number of lattice monopoles is coupled to an additional parameter μ. By tuning β and μ simultaneously, we identify a line of constant physics along which the ratio of the lightest glueball mass to the square root of the string tension remains constant. We further show that the same scaling is satisfied, within numerical uncertainties, by the other low-lying glueball masses considered in this work. Along this trajectory, the string tension in lattice units decreases with increasing β, thus suggesting that the modified lattice action may provide a regularization of three-dimensional compact U(1) gauge theory with a physically well-behaved continuum limit.

    💡 亮点：工作针对三维紧致U(1)晶格规范理论的病态连续极限，引入控制晶格单极子总数的参数μ，并与Wilson耦合β同步调节。目标是建立胶球质量、弦张力等物理量沿线保持不变的连续化路径。该研究与团队的凝聚态材料模拟没有直接材料交叉，但在拓扑缺陷、有效模型参数化和多尺度极限分析上具有有限方法参考。

    二维量子发射体的超分辨率控制#
    Super-resolution Control of Two-dimensional Quantum Emitters#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Bosai Lyu, Valeria Vento, Ludivine Fausten, Daniel Suarez-Forero, Klevis Domi, Kenji Watanabe, Takashi Taniguchi, Alberto Morpurgo, Iaroslav Gaponenko, Patrycja Paruch, Ajit Srivastava🔥 AI 6

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28574v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Localized interlayer excitons in semiconducting transition-metal dichalcogenide heterobilayers are quantum emitters with a static electric dipole moment, making them excellent nanoscale charge sensors to probe correlated quantum phases in a proximal layer. These emitters are electrically tunable and inherit spin-valley selection rules, yet their deterministic spatial control remains challenging due to subwavelength confinement. Here, we present a platform that combines cryogenic optical spectroscopy with scanning probe microscopy to investigate trapped interlayer excitons in WSe₂/MoSe₂ bilayers. By exploiting AFM-based local Stark shift, we achieve super-resolution localization of emitters separated by only a few tens of nanometers and demonstrate deterministic control of individual charge states, including trion formation, opening a path towards coherent inter-dot coupling. Time-resolved measurements reveal tip-induced modification of the electromagnetic vacuum around individual emitters, thus controlling their radiative emission. Our multi-point charge sensing platform with optical readout is particularly well-suited to study fractionalization and anyon dynamics in semiconducting FCIs.

    💡 亮点：局域层间激子同时具备静态电偶极矩和自旋-谷选择定则，可感知邻近层的关联量子相。作者将低温光学光谱与扫描探针显微镜结合，解决亚波长局域发射体的确定性空间控制问题。该平台与团队在二维半导体、范德华异质结、界面电荷转移和激发态动力学方面高度交叉，尤其适合连接GW-BSE与非绝热动力学。

    狄拉克超导体中的规范不变性、集体模与正常态扣除#
    Gauge invariance, collective modes, and the justification of normal-state subtraction in Dirac superconductors#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Hiroshi Hayasaka🔥 AI 6
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：讨论低能狄拉克模型无界谱导致的深能级非物理带间电磁响应。文章分析超导态与正常态响应相减的正常态扣除方法，并研究其与满足Ward恒等式所需顶角修正之间的关系，以及规范不变电磁响应和集体模的联系。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28485v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In Dirac superconductors, the unbounded spectrum of low-energy Dirac models is known to give rise to unphysical interband contributions from deep-lying states to the electromagnetic response. To eliminate these contributions, normal-state subtraction (NSS), in which the normal-state response is subtracted from the superconducting-state response, has been widely employed. However, the relation between NSS and a gauge-invariant electromagnetic response, particularly the role of the vertex correction required by the Ward identity, has remained unclear. In this work, we consider a massive-Dirac model with s-wave pairing and analytically investigate the electromagnetic response at zero temperature by solving the Bethe--Salpeter equation and incorporating the collective-mode contribution to the electromagnetic vertex. We show that, in the static long-wavelength limit, the vertex correction exactly cancels the bare longitudinal response with NSS, yielding the vanishing longitudinal response required by gauge invariance. In contrast, the transverse component of the vertex correction vanishes in the long-wavelength limit, so that the gauge-invariant Meissner weight coincides with that obtained from the bare transverse response with NSS. Moreover, within the class of isotropic and analytic UV regularization terms, we show that gauge invariance uniquely fixes the regularization term in the static long-wavelength limit to the value prescribed by NSS. Our results thus provide a microscopic justification for NSS.

    💡 亮点：文章重新审视狄拉克超导体中正常态扣除的理论依据，核心问题是无界低能谱产生的深能级带间响应如何被消除。作者把正常态扣除与Ward恒等式要求的顶角修正、规范不变性及集体模联系起来。该工作与团队的BdG、Wannier和超导电子结构方法有直接理论交叉，但摘要未给出具体材料或可计算参数。

    滑移自旋链中的长寿命记忆#
    Long-lived memory in sliding spin chains#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Charles Stahl, Ethan Lake
    相关度
    6

    📄 摘要：我们研究了一个由两条铁磁性一维伊辛链构成的系统；该系统与热浴耦合，并通过以恒定速度彼此滑动而被驱动至非平衡态。我们表明，即使在中等速度下，两条链之间的磁摩擦也会显著提高系统形成有序态的能力。具体而言，在逆温度为 β、伊辛耦合为 J、滑动速度为 v 时，系统动力学保留其初始磁化记忆的时间会从 v=0 时的 exp(O(βJ))，增大至当 v>v_c 时的 exp(O((βJ)²v))，其中 v_c 是一个较小常数。因此，磁摩擦提供了一种能够以参数化方式减缓一维磁体热化的简单机制。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2607.14383v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We study a system of two ferromagnetic one-dimensional Ising chains coupled to a thermal bath, which are driven out of equilibrium by being moved past one another at a constant speed. We show that even at modest speeds, magnetic friction between the two chains significantly increases the ability of the system to order. In particular, at inverse temperature β, Ising coupling J, and sliding speed v, the dynamics retains memory of its initial magnetization for a time that increases from exp(O(β J)) at v = 0 to exp(O((b J)²v)) when v>v_c, where v_c is a small constant. Magnetic friction thus provides a simple mechanism for parametrically slowing down thermalization in a one-dimensional magnet.

    💡 亮点：两条相互滑移的铁磁一维Ising链在恒速驱动下表现出增强的磁有序和长寿命初始磁化记忆。磁摩擦使记忆时间从静止时的exp(O(βJ))跨越临界速度vc后变为exp(O((βJ)²v))。该结果为驱动界面中的非平衡磁记忆提供了清晰模型，但与团队真实二维范德华磁体、交换参数和自旋-晶格动力学之间仍只有理论启发关系。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    Flux-Floquet Instability in Fluctuating Superconductors#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Phys. Rev. X👤 Marios H. Michael, Duilio De Santis, Eugene A. Demler, and Patrick A. Lee🔥 AI 84

    📄 摘要：作者：Marios H. Michael、Duilio De Santis、Eugene A. Demler 和 Patrick A. Lee。强太赫兹光在高温超导体中触发磁通-Floquet 不稳定性，产生巨大的磁响应，并揭示束缚电子对在远高于超导转变温度时仍然存在。[《Physical Review X》16，031055]，发表于 2026 年 8 月 31 日。

    📖 英文原文

    Author(s): Marios H. Michael, Duilio De Santis, Eugene A. Demler, and Patrick A. Lee Intense terahertz light triggers a flux-Floquet instability in high-temperature superconductors, generating a giant magnetic response that reveals bound electron pairs survive well above the superconducting transition temperature. [Phys. Rev. X 16, 031055] Published Mon Aug 31, 2026

    💡 亮点：强太赫兹驱动可在涨落超导体中触发通量-Floquet不稳定性，并产生巨大的磁响应。作者将这一非平衡磁响应解释为高温超导转变温度以上仍存续的束缚电子对的证据。该问题与团队的超导、有限磁场效应、BdG和非绝热动力学高度相关，但摘要未给出具体材料、驱动频率、场强或定量响应幅度。

    用浅层递归解码器实时监测MHD液态金属流#
    Real-Time Monitoring of MHD Liquid Metal Flows with Shallow Recurrent Decoders#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Claudio Scardino, Stefano Riva, Carolina Introini, Matteo Lo Verso, Eric Cervi, Antonio Cammi, Laura Savoldi🔥 AI 98
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：针对托卡马克液态金属包层的实时状态估计，构建结合主成分分析和浅层递归解码器SHRED的数据驱动降阶模型。模型将稀疏温度测量映射到完整热-流体状态，以降低高保真多物理场模拟的实时计算成本。摘要截断，未给出误差和推理速度。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28366v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: State estimation in magnetohydrodynamic flows is critical for real-time monitoring of liquid metal blankets in tokamak fusion reactors. Due to the multiphysics nature of these phenomena, high-fidelity simulations are computationally prohibitive for real-time applications. This work investigates a data- driven Reduced Order Model framework: the Shallow Recurrent Decoder (SHRED) coupled with Principal Component Analysis, to map sparse temperature measurements to the full thermo-hydraulic system's state. The major contribution of this work lies in the two-parameter analysis of a fully three-dimensional domain representative of the DEMO breeding blanket configuration. Here, the flow is subjected to an external magnetic field varying in direction and intensity and is hindered by two cylinders acting as a water-cooling system, which impose a temperature boundary condition on their surfaces. This double-parametric magnetic variation induces nonlinear transitions in the flow dynamics, ranging from chaotic behavior at low magnetic field intensities to laminarized regimes at high intensities, characterized by the formation of asymmetric side layers at an inclination angle of 30 degrees. SHRED reconstruction maintains a mean relative error of approximately 5% for the temperature, pressure, and velocity fields. This accuracy is maintained across both weak and strong magnetic fields, ranging from 0.075 T to 0.300 T, and for inclination angles from 5 to 30 degrees, reflecting its dominant toroidal component. These errors are only slightly larger than the lower error bound dictated by low-rank truncation. The results establish SHRED as a reliable state estimator for complex and realistic engineering applications involving completely unseen parametric scenarios and validate it as an accurate real-time state estimation technique suitable for online monitoring and control of real facilities.

    💡 亮点：SHRED与主成分分析结合，将稀疏温度传感器数据重构为液态金属包层的完整热-流体状态，用于托卡马克MHD流动实时监测。核心是低维潜在表示加浅层递归解码器，而非直接求解高保真多物理方程。该方法与团队的主动学习、物理约束机器学习和大尺度动力学模拟具有方法交叉，但不涉及材料电子结构或缺陷机制。

    用于空间天气风险评估的可复现输电基础设施制图方法#
    A Reproducible Method for Mapping Electricity Transmission Infrastructure for Space Weather Risk Assessment#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Dennies K. Bor, Edward J. Oughton, Evan A. Peters, Noah Rivera, C. Trevor Gaunt, Robert Weigel, Michael Wiltberger
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：针对地磁感应电流建模缺少输电资产信息的问题，提出基于开放数据的系统制图流程。方法从OpenStreetMap变电站位置出发，利用浏览器标注平台构建高分辨率、部件级输电设施图；初始样本包含1313座电压不低于230 kV的变电站，摘要后续数量截断。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2412.17685v3 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Space weather risk assessment is constrained by the lack of available asset information needed to model geomagnetically induced currents (GICs) in electricity transmission infrastructure. We propose a systematic method that enables risk analysts to collect their own open-source substation data. Using a web browser platform for annotation, we convert OpenStreetMap (OSM) substation locations into high-resolution, component-level mappings of electricity transmission assets. We convert an initial 1,313 high-voltage (>=230 kV) substations to 52,273 components using low-altitude, satellite, and Street View imagery accessed through Google Earth, identifying 7,949 transformers. Compared to the OSM baseline, this approach provides detailed insights on voltage levels and substation configurations. We then construct a geospatial GIC network for the Tennessee Valley Authority (TVA) region, comparing May 2024 results with the University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign 150-bus (UIUC150) synthetic network and with measured ground GICs at 13 monitoring devices. The transformer types at unannotated substations and the grounding resistances are unknown, so we sample both across a Monte Carlo ensemble. This gives a median TVA 95th-percentile peak ground GIC of 29.1 A, with a 90 percent confidence interval of 23.9-36.1 A. The UIUC150 network yields a 95th-percentile peak ground GIC of 35.8 A under the same forcing, falling within this interval, and the modeled time series broadly capture the temporal morphology of the geomagnetic storm at the monitoring sites. This method shows promise for spatially explicit, screening-level GIC assessment without requiring access to operator data.

    💡 亮点：文章建立了从OpenStreetMap变电站位置到部件级输电设施图的可复现浏览器标注流程，用于空间天气引起的地磁感应电流风险评估。初始数据覆盖1313座不低于230 kV的变电站，后续扩展数量在摘要中被截断。它与团队材料模拟没有直接物理交叉，但在开放数据、可复现流程和大规模风险建模方面具有有限AI for science方法参考。

    TinyML中的电压与电磁故障注入攻击及防御#
    Voltage and Electromagnetic Fault Injectioin in TinyML: Attacks and Countermeasures#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Preprints👤 Srilalith Nampally, Anthony Etim, Aubtin Rasouli, Dustin Mazza, Tinghung Chiu, Ferhat Erata 等9位作者🔥 AI 18
    相关度
    0

    📄 摘要：研究资源受限微控制器上的TinyML模型面对电压毛刺和电磁故障注入时的脆弱性。通过系统实验改变故障注入配置，评估模型计算错误和可靠性风险，并讨论针对物理攻击的防御措施。摘要未给出具体芯片、模型架构、注入参数或防御效果数值。

    📖 英文原文

    Tiny Machine Learning (TinyML) algorithms, designed to operate on constrained devices such as those found in Internet of Things (IoT) systems, are vulnerable to adversarial threats, including fault injection attacks. These attacks exploit physical means to induce errors in computation, compromising the reliability of the device and the TinyML models running on it. This work investigates the operation of TinyML models under fault injection attacks. Through systematic experimentation with voltage glitching and EM fault injection attacks on microcontrollers, this work identifiesconfigurations that adversaries can exploit to induce faults without triggering a system reset, thus focusing on finding the more stealthy attacks. This study analyzes four types of TinyML models, and demonstrates that all four evaluated TinyML models will generate inference outputs with reduced accuracy under the two types of fault injection attacks. Further, in some instances, this work shows that it may be feasible for the attackers to use the faults to cause inference operations to return a predictable output, not just random incorrect inference results. This highlights the need for more robust fault injection protection mechanisms in TinyML implementations. In order to provide one such protection, this work demonstrates the use of Randomized Self-Reduction (RSR) schemes and majority voting for intermediate values as a means to protect the TinyML models.

    💡 亮点：文章在微控制器上系统考察电压毛刺和电磁故障注入对TinyML推理的影响，关注物理攻击如何诱发计算错误并破坏模型可靠性。工作重点是实验配置、攻击条件和防御策略，而非材料性质预测。它与团队的材料机器学习只有部署鲁棒性层面的间接关联，不能据此推断等变网络、机器学习势或Kohn-Sham哈密顿量在类似攻击下的具体行为。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《TinyML中的电压与电磁故障注入攻击及防御》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    太阳磁场构型对辐射带电子的调控#
    Solar Magnetic Configuration Control over Radiation Belt Electrons#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Ahmad Lalti, Jonathan Rae, Clare Watt, Stephanie Yardley, Savvas Raptis🔥 AI 92
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：研究地球外辐射带电子在太阳周期、季节和27天Carrington时间尺度上的变化。文章挑战仅用局地几何效应解释季节变化的观点，指出周期性重复的日冕洞太阳风同样可驱动季节尺度的辐射带变化。摘要未给出具体观测数据量或模型参数。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28309v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Earth is surrounded by two highly dynamic concentric belts of particle radiation. The outer radiation belt exhibits coherent variability at solar-cycle, seasonal, and 27-day (Carrington) timescales. While the solar-cycle and Carrington variations have been attributed to the recurrence of coronal hole solar wind, the seasonal variation has long been explained by local geometric effects that modulate the solar wind-magnetosphere coupling. Here, we challenge this paradigm by showing that the periodic recurrence of coronal hole solar wind likewise drives the seasonal variation. We further demonstrate that the Alfvénic nature of this solar wind is responsible for the observed electron flux enhancement in the outer radiation belt. These findings provide a unifying framework linking solar magnetic topology, solar wind properties, and magnetospheric dynamics across multiple timescales at Earth and beyond.

    💡 亮点：研究指出，外辐射带电子的季节性变化不仅来自太阳风-磁层耦合的局地几何调制，周期性重复的日冕洞太阳风也能产生相应驱动。分析同时覆盖太阳周期、季节和27天Carrington尺度。该工作与团队材料研究没有直接交叉，仅在外场驱动、磁场环境和多时间尺度动力学方面存在非常间接的方法联系。

    横向磁场中自然对流准二维模型的验证#
    Validation of quasi-two-dimensional model of convection in a transverse magnetic field#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Alexander Gelfgat🔥 AI 89
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：验证横向磁场中矩形容器自然对流的准二维模型。将Q2D模型与完整三维计算比较，考察方形截面和长宽比为8的水平延伸容器，并把宽高比从1变化到10。结果认为模型在包括理想热边界等特定条件下有效，摘要未完整列出条件。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28023v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: In this study we examine applicability of the quasi-two-dimensional (Q2D) model of natural convection in a laterally heated rectangular box subject to a transverse magnetic field. For this purpose, results obtained using the Q2D model are compared with fully three-dimensional calculations for rectangular boxes with a square cross-section and horizontally elongated ones with the length to height ratio 8. The width to height ratio is varied from 1 to 10. It is argued that the Q2D model is valid under certain conditions, which include perfect thermal and electrical insulation of the spanwise boundaries and reflection symmetry with respect to the spanwise midplane. It is shown that under these conditions, the Q2D model yields quantitatively correct results for boxes with square cross-section, as well as for horizontally elongated cavities, independently on the width to height ratio. Three-dimensional results obtained for boxes with perfectly conducting walls exhibit asymptotics at gradually increasing width ratio, which, however, does not agree with the predictions of an amended Q2D model. It is shown also that when is applicable, the Q2D model can correctly predict steady-oscillatory transition of three-dimensional flows. It is proposed to seek for extensions of the Q2D model for cavities with perfectly conducting spanwise boundaries.

    💡 亮点：文章用完整三维计算检验横向磁场中自然对流的准二维Q2D近似，覆盖方形截面及长度高度比为8的长盒，并扫描宽度高度比1至10。结论是模型只在特定条件下有效，理想热边界是条件之一。该工作与团队材料模拟没有直接交叉，但对热输运、磁流体和降阶模型验证提供有限方法参考。

    托卡马克等离子体中中高模数电磁压强梯度不稳定性#
    Electromagnetic pressure-gradient-driven instabilities with moderate high mode numbers in tokamak plasmas#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Yann Narbutt, Ksenia Aleynikova, Matthias Borchardt, Thomas Hayward-Schneider, Ralf Kleiber, Axel Könies, Alexey Mishchenko, Carolin Nührenberg, Edilberto Sánchez🔥 AI 62
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：使用全局回旋动理学代码EUTERPE研究托卡马克中的压强梯度驱动不稳定性，重点关注中高模数。固定MHD几何时，归一化压强β升高会从离子温度梯度模突变到动力学气球模；若每个β都重新计算自洽平衡磁场，转变行为会改变。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.28367v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Pressure gradient-driven instabilities are investigated in tokamak plasmas using the global gyrokinetic code EUTERPE emphasizing the role of moderate high mode numbers. As the normalized plasma pressure β is increased, there is a well-known, sudden transition from ion-temperature-gradient (ITG) instabilities to kinetic ballooning modes (KBM), if the magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) geometry is held fixed. However, if the equilibrium field is recomputed for each value of β, so that the equilibrium is consistent with the stability calculation, the transition can disappear. In a number of cases, we are only able to find an ITG-KBM transition if inconsistent equilibria are used. In the MHD unstable regime, gyrokinetic simulations and MHD stability calculations show good agreement for moderate ratios of ion temperature gradient to density gradient and small values of the ion gyro-radius. Otherwise, non-MHD contributions are important, such as the diamagnetic stabilization and ion wave--particle resonant effects.

    💡 亮点：EUTERPE全局回旋动理学计算显示，固定平衡几何时升高β可触发ITG到KBM的突变，而对每个β重新求解自洽平衡后，转变行为发生变化。研究特别强调中高模数电磁不稳定性的作用。与团队直接交叉有限，主要可借鉴自洽背景场、参数扫描和多尺度稳定性分析方法，体系本身不属于凝聚态材料。

    用于HSX仿星器原位磁场监测的AlGaN/GaN霍尔传感器#
    AlGaN/GaN Hall-Effect Sensor for In-Situ Magnetic Field Monitoring of the HSX Stellarator#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 arXiv / physics👤 Yiming Zhao, Wayne Goodman, Thomas Gallenberger, Jasmine M. Cox, Benedikt Geiger, Debbie G. Senesky
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：制备、封装并部署AlGaN/GaN霍尔传感器，在HSX准螺旋对称仿星器等离子体边缘原位监测磁场。HSX轴上磁场为1 T，电子回旋共振加热功率最高200 kW。直接磁场传感器用于缓解传统感应式诊断的积分漂移，摘要未给出传感器灵敏度和耐辐照数据。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.26704v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Direct magnetic field sensors can address integration drift commonly observed in conventional inductive magnetic diagnostics used in fusion systems. In this work, an AlGaN/GaN Hall-effect sensor was fabricated, packaged, and deployed inside the Helically Symmetric eXperiment (HSX)---the first quasi-helically symmetric stellarator, operating with a 1 T on-axis magnetic field and up to 200 kW of launched electron cyclotron resonance heating (ECRH) power---for in-situ magnetic field monitoring near the plasma edge. The sensor leverages the high-mobility two-dimensional electron gas (2DEG) formed in the AlGaN/GaN heterostructure for sensitive magnetic field measurement, while the wide-bandgap GaN material system provides thermal robustness for harsh-environment operation. During 68 consecutive plasma discharge shots, the sensor remained functional and produced clear transient responses associated with plasma ignition and discharge dynamics. Comparisons between biased and unbiased operation, as well as plasma-discharge and coil-only shots, confirmed that the response originated from the biased Hall-effect sensor element. Furthermore, the sensor output exhibited temporal correlation with the plasma stored energy measured by the HSX diamagnetic loop across high-energy, late-breakdown, and failed-breakdown discharges.

    💡 亮点：AlGaN/GaN霍尔传感器被封装并部署到HSX仿星器等离子体边缘，实现原位磁场监测。装置运行在1 T轴向磁场和最高200 kW电子回旋共振加热条件下，目标是消除感应诊断的积分漂移。该文与团队在GaN、半导体缺陷、输运和器件材料方面有直接材料交叉，但摘要没有报告缺陷计算或机器学习内容。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：它与五位研究人员已有工作的直接连接是：磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构已经出现在团队的方向画像中，可对应到CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物。与传统只做 0 K formation energy/静态势垒的工作相比，这里更应关注磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 声子、有限温度和输运性质, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系在温度、缺陷、应变或外场下的变化；摘要没有给出的模型细节不作延伸判断。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对当前研究最具体的启示不是泛泛地“使用 ML”，而是把该文的磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构嵌入现有材料模拟链：先在CrI₃/CrSBr 等范德华磁体、反铁磁/交替磁材料、磁性氧化物中选择一个已有 DFT 数据基础的体系，按“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”补充训练/验证集，再比较《用于HSX仿星器原位磁场监测的AlGaN/GaN霍尔传感器》对应模型对相稳定、极化/磁序、缺陷或动力学指标的改进。若结果只在训练分布内有效，应通过跨温度、跨应变、跨缺陷浓度和跨超胞测试检查可迁移性。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    Electrically Conductive but Thermally Insulating PPy‐Coated Nonwoven Fabric for Wearable Thermal Management, Electromagnetic Interference Shielding, and Sensing Applications#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态P2 · 磁性·自旋电子学📖 Wiley👤 Baoxin Fan, Kang Fu, Cheng Xie, Junye Liao, Junzhe Shang, Liyan Xie, Zidong He, Tong Wu, Guoxiu Tong🔥 AI 1
    相关度
    5.5

    📄 摘要：《Advanced Functional Materials》，EarlyView。该工作研究一种兼具导电性和热绝缘性的聚吡咯涂覆非织造织物，并探讨其在可穿戴热管理、电磁干扰屏蔽和传感应用中的潜力，具体研究内容与结论需查阅原文。
